PSO ROUTE GRAPH, DROP ROUTES, AND SAFETY STATES
===============================================

PURPOSE
Machine-readable route corpus converted to text: platform definitions, route nodes/edges, 40 imported route rows, MAG route data, route safety states, and hunter goal engine metadata. Current packaged drop database outranks legacy route rows if they disagree.

SOURCE BASIS
pso-routegraph.json and pso-drop-routes.json from Hunter Goals v0.3.

RETRIEVAL RULE
Treat explicit platform labels, verification/status fields, source labels, and warnings as part of the fact. Never silently remove them.
